- **H-check CORPUS-driven conformance (the durable harness)** — pivots self-host validation off bespoke snippets onto the REAL, implementation-agnostic corpus (`tests/cases/**/*.vl` + `@directive`s), whose own header promises it "will validate a future self-hosted compiler unchanged." `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts` drives a WHITELIST of corpus files through the VL front end (`lexer.vl → parser.vl → typecheck.vl`) and asserts VL's VERDICT matches the spec: an `@error` file must be REJECTED (≥1 parse/type diagnostic), any other ACCEPTED (0 diagnostics) — the directive is the oracle, so no TS run and no fragile message-text comparison (VL's diagnostic WORDING differs from TS's; text+position parity needs source-span threading, a later milestone). Seeded at **17 files** (8 `@error` type errors the checker raises + 9 clean programs it checks cleanly), discovered by a sweep over candidate dirs; the count is the conformance ledger and grows as coverage lands. Enabler: `typecheck.vl` now knows the `print` builtin (a `print(x)` Call special-case → void, args still checked) so printing programs don't false-error. SCOPE today is typecheck-verdict only — emit/`@run` log cross-check awaits `print` EMISSION in `wasmEmit.vl`; `@error-at L:C` awaits span threading. The bespoke snippet tests become transitional, retiring as the corpus whitelist absorbs their coverage. (`compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.4: LOOPS in `typecheck.vl` + corpus ledger 17 → 50** — `checkNode` now type-checks `while` (condition must be `bool`, body checked) and accepts `break`/`continue` (loop-nesting not tracked yet — a misplaced one is a parser concern). With loops checkable, a wider corpus sweep grew the conformance whitelist from 17 to **50 files**: 31 clean programs VL parses + checks with zero diagnostics (arith, `while`-loops, globals/struct-field mutation, mutual recursion + forward references incl. struct params, definite-assignment, object/array equality, empty-array usage inference) + 19 genuine front-end rejections (type errors the checker raises: assign/return/arg-count/arg-type/condition/boolean-coercion mismatches; lexer errors: malformed hex/separators; parser errors: illegal commas). The reject set is curated to rejections VL makes for a SPEC-ALIGNED reason — advanced-feature `@error` files (negation/intersection/recursive-types/null-inference/lambdas) are excluded as parse-subset artifacts. (`compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.5: STRING methods + FULL-CORPUS sweep — ledger 57 → 156** — `checkNode` now type-checks the string methods the compiler's own sources lean on: `.slice(i32, i32) → string`, `.indexOf(string) → i32`, `.includes(string) → bool`, `.charCodeAt(i32) → i32` (string-receiver cases in the call path, args checked; `.length` stays a Member, `s[i]` an Index), plus the `fromCodePoint(i32) → string` builtin (Ident special-case alongside `print`). The self-host checker now models every type the emitter does (i32/f64/bool/string/struct/union/array; maps are the last core-vocabulary gap). With that, a **full-corpus sweep** (all 422 single-file cases driven through `lexer.vl → parser.vl → typecheck.vl` in per-file crash isolation) grew the conformance whitelist from 57 to **156 files** — **64 ACCEPTED** (clean programs VL parses + checks with zero diagnostics) + **92 REJECTED** (invalid programs VL refuses: type errors the checker raises, lexer errors, parser syntax errors). Each entry is VL behaving correctly per the directive (verdict conformance; some advanced `@error` files are refused because the construct is outside VL's subset — a correct refusal, and the test self-corrects if a file later flips). The 266 remaining disagreements are the work left: clean files VL can't yet PARSE (`for`-loops, lambdas, generics, `if/then/else` expressions) and `@error` files VL doesn't yet CATCH (redeclaration, const-reassign). (`compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.6: FOR-loops (parser + ast + checker) — ledger 156 → 179** — the single biggest corpus blocker was `for`: 82 valid files used it and the self-host parser had ZERO support. Added two AST nodes — `ForRange` (`for v in A to B [step S] { … }`, a numeric range) and `ForIn` (`for v in arr { … }`, array iteration) — with `mkForRange`/`mkForIn` (`ast.vl`); `parseFor` in `parser.vl` (dispatched from `parseStmt` on the `FOR` keyword; `in`/`to`/`step` are SOFT keywords matched by text, the range-vs-in form decided by whether a soft `to` follows the first expression); and `checkNode` cases in `typecheck.vl` (range bounds + step must be i32, loop var is i32 in the body scope; for-in binds the var to the array's element type and rejects a non-array iterable). Purely additive — no existing self-host test uses `for`, and the compiler's own source is `while`-only, so the golden/fixpoint bytes are unchanged. A re-sweep grew the ledger from 156 to **179** (+23 valid `for` programs now conform; the `for` `@error` files — non-i32 bound, for-in over a non-array — are correctly rejected by the new checks). (`compiler/ast.vl`, `compiler/parser.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.7: inline UNION type annotations — ledger 179 → 182** — the largest remaining parser blocker was `|` in type position (`x: A | B`, `T | null`): the self-host parser only did `type N = A | B` decls, not inline annotations. `parseTypeName` now parses a `|`-chain of type atoms into a synthetic name `"A|B|null"` (mirroring the `i32[]`/`{[K]:V}` synthetic names; `null` is accepted as a member; safe because `parseTypeName` is only reached in type position, where `|` is a union separator not the bitwise-OR operator, and `parseTypeDecl` parses its own variants). `nameToTy` splits the synthetic name on `|` (checked BEFORE the `[]`/map shape so a member's suffix isn't misread) and builds a `TyUnion` (or `TyNullable` when a `null` member is present), reusing the existing union/nullable `assignable`. Net +3 (re-sweep: +6 valid union-using programs conform; −3 `@error` union files that now PARSE but whose error VL doesn't yet catch are honestly dropped). COMPLEMENT (negative) narrowing — refining `x` to its type-minus-`T` after a diverging `if x is T { … return }` — was implemented and REVERTED: plain `is T` complement made VL MISS real errors in soundness files that need richer flow narrowing (`!(x is T)`, `== null`, value-kind unions), a net verdict regression, so it is deliberately deferred until done properly. (`compiler/parser.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H3 codegen-bug pay-down: the workaround-forcing host bugs are FIXED — pinned + de-contorted** — the four historical host miscompiles that forced the self-host `.vl` sources into workaround style were re-tested and are ALL resolved by intervening host fixes: (1) two functions `is`-narrowing the SAME union, mutually recursive (the "Finding 2" mono-visitor constraint); (2) a per-variant helper taking the NARROWED variant struct and recursing back; (3) a struct-typed value passed into a mutually-recursive function group ("Unhandled AST → WASM Never"); (4) `.pop()` discarded at statement position on a struct-field array + a discarded/`let`-bound call return at module scale (PR #4/#5 gaps). Each is pinned by a new corpus case (`types/union-two-visitors.vl`, `types/union-narrowed-helper-recursion.vl`, `functions/struct-param-mutual-recursion-global.vl`, `lists/struct-field-pop-statement.vl`, `statements/discarded-call-return.vl`). The `typecheck.vl` mega-visitor is then DE-CONTORTED as the module-scale proof: the `Call`/`FuncDecl`/`Program` arms move out of `checkNode` into natural per-kind helpers (`checkCallNode(n: Call, ix)`, `checkFuncDeclNode(n: FuncDecl)`, `checkProgramNode(n: Program)`) that take the narrowed variant and recurse back — four functions now narrow the `Node` union in the largest concatenated module and everything passes (corpus 182/182, check→emit, pipeline). `popScope` drops the `_dropped` binding; `checkProgram` drops the `_root` indirection (its own body now exercises the discarded-call shape). Stale gap notes in `ast.vl`/`parser.vl`/`typecheck.vl` updated — `P`/`T` as globals is now a documented STYLE choice, not a forced workaround. This unblocks writing all future self-host code in natural, idiomatic shape (the Phase A step-1 of the self-hosting plan). (`compiler/typecheck.vl`, `compiler/ast.vl`, `compiler/parser.vl`, `tests/cases/…`)
- **H-check Phase 2.8: bare-expression bodies + RETURN-TYPE INFERENCE — ledger 182 → 219** — the "inference everywhere" idiom lands in the self-host front end. `parser.vl`: a function with no `{` after its signature (`function dbl(x: i32) x * 2`) parses its single expression into a synthetic one-statement Block (the same shape as a braced trailing-expression body). `typecheck.vl`: an UN-ANNOTATED return type is INFERRED — the function hoists with a `-2` PENDING sentinel; checking its body accumulates witnesses (each `return e` and the trailing expression, via `noteInferredRet` under the pending `curRet`; first concrete type wins, an in-cycle call contributes only "err"); the result is PATCHED into the hoisted `TyFunc` IN PLACE so every caller sees it. A forward call to a not-yet-inferred callee triggers DEMAND inference (`checkCallNode` checks the callee's FuncDecl via the new `fnDeclIx` name→decl map, with an `inferStack` in-progress guard; pass-2 then skips the already-inferred body so diagnostics aren't duplicated). A base-case-less cycle (`ping(){return pong()}` ↔ `pong(){return ping()}`) has no concrete witness and reports "cannot infer a return type — annotate" (matching the host's A17 contract); a self-recursive function WITH a base case infers fine (the in-cycle call types as the err hole, the base case provides the witness). Re-sweep: **+37 files** (the `functions/*` bucket — bare bodies, nested fns, forward/mutual recursion with inference — plus inference-dependent strays). `++`/`--` was considered and deliberately SKIPPED: a parse-time desugar would bake wrong post-increment value semantics into the AST (debt); proper inc/dec nodes come with emit support. (`compiler/parser.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.9: MAPS + SETS in `typecheck.vl` — ledger 219 → 229** — the last core-vocabulary gap closes: the self-host checker now models string-keyed maps and sets. A `TyMap { mKey, mVal, mSet }` arena variant — `mSet` distinguishes the SET flavor (a set is internally a boolean-valued map but exposes ONLY `.add`/`.has`/`.delete`/`.length`/`.values()`; the Map surface — `.set`/`.get`/`.keys` — must not leak, per the B6a/C2.2 regression cases). `nameToTy` resolves the parser's synthetic `{[K]:V}` index-signature name (splitting at the first `]:` so a nested-map VALUE parses; non-string keys resolve to "unknown type" — the host's rep). `Map()`/`Set()` construct HOLE maps (key/value `-2`… `-1`) pinned by the binding annotation or the FIRST `.set`/`.add` (usage inference, mutating the TyMap in place — a later conflicting use is a clean type error, the `error-infer-conflict` cases). A `Set()` initializer keeps its set flavor under a map-shaped annotation. Index `m[k]` checks the key and yields the NULLABLE value (`V | null`, defaulted with the new `??` nullish-coalescing case: a nullable LHS strips to its inner type, the default checked against it); `.length` → i32; map `.keys()`/`.values()` → `K[]`/`V[]`; set `.values()` → its ELEMENTS (`K[]`). Re-sweep: +13 accepts (the `maps/`/`sets/` behavior files: basics, delete/churn, iteration-order, infer-from-set, many-keys) and recovers 4 rejects (infer-conflict ×2, set `.get`/`.set` leak). The remaining map disagreements need the scope-close "cannot infer" floor (deferred). (`compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.10: inline OBJECT types + `null` + NULL-NARROWING + `?.` — ledger 229 → 245** — three thematically-one pieces of the soundness slice. (1) INLINE object type annotations `{ x: i32 }` (anonymous structural types, e.g. a `{ x: i32 } | null` return): `parseTypeAtom`'s `{` branch now forks on the next token — `[` stays the map `{[K]:V}`, an IDENT parses a field list into the synthetic name `"{x:i32,y:string}"`; `nameToTy` splits it back (TOP-LEVEL commas, each field at its first top-level `:`) into a structural `TyObj`, and the union `|`-split / map split became DEPTH-AWARE so a field's own union/map type doesn't shred the outer name. (2) The `null` LITERAL: a `NullLit` AST node, a `parsePrimary` case, a `TY_NULL` interned prim, and `assignable`'s nullable-target rule accepts it. (3) NULL-NARROWING over a simple identifier: `if x != null { … }` refines a nullable `x` to its inner type in the THEN branch; `if x == null { … } else { … }` refines the ELSE; and a diverging then (`if x == null { return … }` — return/break/continue last) makes `x` non-null for the REST of the block (the guard-clause idiom). Conservative null-stripping only — the union-complement lesson held: REJECTS stayed flat through the change (no newly-missed errors). Plus `?.` OPTIONAL CHAINING: an additive `OptMember` node (`o?.field` — no `Member` shape churn), a `parsePostfix` case over the lexer's existing `QUESTION_DOT`, and a checker arm that reads the field off a nullable receiver's INNER type and re-wraps the result nullable (`({x:i32}|null)?.x` → `i32|null`, defaulted with `??`). Re-sweep: +17 accepts (soundness/types null-guard files, map-of-struct `?.` files), −1 reject. (`compiler/ast.vl`, `compiler/parser.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.11: FUNCTION GENERICS `<T>` — ledger 245 → 251** — function type parameters with genuine CALL-SITE inference. `FuncDecl` gains `fnTyParams: string[]` (`parseFunc` collects `<T, U>` after the name — declaration position only, no `<`-comparison ambiguity). A new `TyVar { tvName }` arena variant: OPAQUE inside the generic body (assignable both ways, numeric-permissive — like the err hole, so `id<T>(x: T): T x` checks), but at a CALL SITE each TyVar param (or `T[]`) BINDS its type-param to the argument's type — first use wins, later uses must agree — and the RETURN substitutes through the binding (`resolveAnnot` maps a param/return annotation through the fn's type-param list before `nameToTy`). So `id("s")` IS `string`, and the correlation `@error` files (`let n: i32 = id("s")`, `first(["s"])`) remain genuinely REJECTED — rejects stayed flat through the change. Re-sweep: +6 accepts (`generics/{identity,first,last,swap,trailing-comma,generic-array-passthrough}`-class files). Generic `type` ALIASES (`type Box<T>`) stay out of scope (their `@error` arity files keep failing at parse, correctly). (`compiler/ast.vl`, `compiler/parser.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H nullable struct refs end-to-end + checker flow-narrowing collectors — runtime oracle 113 → 119** — `S | null` (a declared name or an inline `{x:i32}` shape) lands as emitter kind 9, `(ref null $S)`: params/returns/locals/globals, `ref.is_null` for `== null`/`!= null`/`is null`, seeded `ref.null $S` for a bare `null` value (return/argument context), and `ref.as_non_null` on guarded member reads (the checker proves every access guarded, so the cast never traps on a checked program); `retStructIndex` resolves the nullable BASE as the one member-layout chokepoint, and kind-9 cells skip the non-const global read recast (their null IS the value). The checker's narrowing rewrites onto two condition COLLECTORS (`collectThenNarrows`/`collectElseNarrows`): `&&`/`||` chains (both facts), De Morgan via `!(…)`, `is null`, and `&&`/`||` RHS checked UNDER the left side's facts (`p != null && p.x > 0`); guard clauses apply the false-branch facts to the rest of the block. (`compiler/typecheck.vl`, `compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-front parser/lexer sugar tail + for-loop `continue` fix — runtime oracle 102 → 113** — compound assignment (`+=`/`-=`/`*=`/`/=` desugar to `a = a + (b)`), prefix/postfix `++`/`--` (desugar to `x = x + 1`; postfix-in-VALUE-position old-value semantics deferred), `if cond then <stmt>` single-statement sugar, trailing commas + multi-line forms in array literals and call args, bitwise `^`/`<<` (precedence ladder renumbered to the host's `| < ^ < &` order), and hex/binary/octal literals with `_` digit separators (legal only BETWEEN digits — doubled/trailing separators still reject). The `continue` fix: a for-range/for-in body with a DIRECT `continue` wraps in an INNER block so the branch lands on the increment (branching to the loop header skipped it — an infinite loop); bodies without one keep the golden-pinned shape. The self-host checker gains `const` reassignment rejection (`constScopes`, a parallel per-scope const-name chain; `x++` on a const now errors like the host). (`compiler/lexer.vl`, `compiler/parser.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit `toString` builtin + checker array methods — runtime oracle 90 → 102** — `toString(x)` lands end-to-end: the self-host CHECKER types it (i32/boolean → string) and the EMITTER lowers it inline over the string-op scratch frame — digits build in NEGATIVE space (a positive negates, so i32 MIN needs no overflowing flip; each digit is `48 - (v % 10)` of the negative remainder), a boolean picks "true"/"false" via typed `select` (`0x1c` — an if/else `local.set` of a NON-defaultable ref local does not count as initialization past the `end`). The checker gains array `.pop()` / `.get(i)` (both `T | null`) and `.clear()`. Start-fn scratch detection now descends TOP-LEVEL `if` chains (extracted `startScanBlock`/`startScanIf`). Anonymous shapes get synthetic `#anonN` names (the empty string is `rlInternName`'s "not a ref element" sentinel, so a ref list of anon-shape elements never interned — an OOB trap); the `fbValtype` family guards table OVERRUNS too. (`compiler/typecheck.vl`, `compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit `for…in` + map surface + uninitialized locals — runtime oracle 82 → 90** — `for v in <iter>` lowers over an i32 list, a `string[]` list, or a map's ordered `.keys()`/`.values()` (three pre-order cursor slots: element var, hidden i32 cursor, hidden list temp — the iterable evaluates ONCE; every scratch/locals walker recurses into `fiBody`); the start fn collects locals BEFORE scratch detection (the detectors classify through the declared-locals registry — `print(k)` over a for-in string element needs `k`'s slot). Map surface: `.length` (the live `size` field), the `.set(k, v)` statement, GLOBAL map receivers (`globalIsMap`), un-annotated `Map()` (type collection + `globalKind` kind 6), `.has` printing true/false, and a LOUD failure for ref-valued sets. Uninitialized `let x: i32` (A-definite-assign) emits as a zero-init slot. String-element array literals force the string-list types (`collectA` + `globalKind` kind 7); `names[i]` classifies as a string (`exprString` Index case). (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit anonymous struct shapes + trap pay-down — runtime oracle 71 → 82, emitter traps 12 → 0** — `collectAnonShapes` interns every un-declared object-literal field-name set as an unnamed struct (appended AFTER declared structs, so declared indices — and goldens — are unchanged), unlocking the no-`type` `objects/*` family; `fRetBool` joins the inferred-return tables (un-annotated `isEven`/`isOdd` print true/false); `globalKind` classifies NON-literal global initializers (a concat, `fromCodePoint(..)`, a cross-global ref) via the expression classifiers, fixing the string-global family; a tail-ASSIGNMENT return infers as its RHS. Every remaining unsupported shape now fails LOUDLY instead of trapping: unresolved-slot guards in `fbValtype`/`fbValtypeNullable`/`fbRefNullForKind`/`sFieldIndex`, the start-fn (`fnIx -1`) LetDecl arena read, and a final `emitFailed` check in `emitProgram` that catches a deep helper's swallowed failure. (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit return-type INFERENCE + print(string) — runtime oracle 60 → 71** — two pieces that compose. (1) Un-annotated non-void functions infer their RETURN KIND from the body's return expression (`fnRetExprOf`: the tail value or the first valued `return`, walking if/while/for): `computeRetInference` classifies it with the expression classifiers (`exprString`/`exprStruct`/`exprArray`/`exprRefArray`/`exprUnion`) and SETS the SAME `fRet*` tables the annotation path fills — so the functype result valtype AND every call-site classification follow from one place; iterated to a fixed point like voidness (`function outer() { return inner() }` needs inner's inferred kind, and `exprString`'s Call case reads `fRetStr`). (2) `print(string)` streams each code point through `__print_char__` (import 2) then `__print_str_flush__` (import 3) — an inline loop over the string-op scratch frame (A = the ref, I0 = cursor, I1 = `array.len`); the strop detection walkers treat `print(<string>)` as a string op so the frame reserves. Together: `function get() { return strGlobal }` + `print(get())` — the `globals/string-*` family — runs byte-correct. (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit VOID functions — runtime oracle 58 → 60** — `emitProgram` classifies genuinely-void functions (`function f() { print(x) }`: no return annotation, no valued `return`, no tail value) and emits them as `()->()`: a new `fRetVoid` table computed as a MONOTONE FIXED POINT (`computeVoidFns` — `stmtIsTailValue`'s Call case consults `fRetVoid`, so a tail call to a void callee makes the CALLER void too; the void set only grows, bounded by the function count). A void functype emits 0 results; a void body skips the fallthrough-`unreachable` padding (empty-stack fallthrough is valid — this was the RUNTIME-trap source); a statement call to a void function skips the result `drop` (alongside `print`). Un-annotated functions RETURNING refs (a string global) still default their functype to i32 — emitter-side return-type inference is the next slice, with the 11 emitter `unreachable` traps (unguarded paths in `objects/*` methods, map delete). (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit assignment-as-expression — runtime oracle 54 → 58** — assignment yields its RIGHT-HAND SIDE (confirmed deliberate; → `DECISIONS.md`): `emitExpr`'s `=` case lowers an Ident-target assignment as STORE-then-RE-READ (`emitAssign`, then the target re-reads through the normal Ident path with its non-const-ref re-cast logic), so `b = (a = 5)`, `while (a = a - 1) > 0`, and nested-assign conditions all emit; `stmtIsTailValue` admits a trailing Ident-target assignment, so `function bump() { count = count + 1 }` RETURNS the new count via the trailing-expression rule (fixing the "expected 1 elements on the stack" family). The self-host checker's `=` now yields the RHS type (host parity). Index/Member-target assignments stay statements in value position (later slice). ROADMAP B17 gains the user-flagged lints: assignment-of-a-LITERAL in condition position (`if x = true` with a boolean `x` slips past the mandatory-bool check — almost certainly a mistyped `==`) and per-line/per-file diagnostic suppression (`// vl-ignore <code>`). Remaining run-failures: 10 (genuinely-void functions — trailing `print` — the `()->()` functype slice), 2 log-diffs, 11 emitter traps. (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `DECISIONS.md`, `ROADMAP.md`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit start-fn scratch fix + bool prints — runtime oracle 28 → 54** — two fixes nearly double the runtime oracle. (1) The start function's scratch DETECTION used only the `.pop` walker for the push flags; `fnHasPush` uses BOTH (`blockHasPushKind` OR `blockHasMemberPopKind` — a Member-receiver pop reuses the push frame). The top-level-statement scan now mirrors it exactly (block walkers for `for`/`while` bodies, `stmtIsPushKind` + the expr walkers for bare statements, plus ref-push SLOT recording via `refListSlotOfExpr`), so a top-level `out.push(i)` (the `lists/build-fusion` family) reserves its quad — fixing ~24 "invalid local index" modules. (2) `print(x)` now calls `__print_bool__` (import 1, renders true/false) when the arg is BOOLEAN-classified — `exprIsBool`: bool literals, comparisons, logical connectives, `!`, `is` guards, parens, and calls to a `boolean`-annotated function; identifiers stay unclassified (annotation threading later). Remaining run-failures: 14 (void functions — `function f() { … }` with no return value — the next emit feature), 2 log-diffs (inferred-bool returns; f64), 11 emitter traps. (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-emit FOR-RANGE lowering + start-fn locals — runtime oracle 24 → 28** — `emitProgram` lowers `for v in A to B [step K] { … }` (INCLUSIVE range) to the same block/loop shape as `while`: the loop var and a synthetic bound temp (`v#b` — the `to` expression evaluates ONCE) own the next two cursor slots (`collectLocals` allocates them in the same pre-order, mirrored by the emit-side cursor); the step must be a CONSTANT integer literal (optionally negated) and its SIGN picks the exit comparison (`i32.le_s` ascending / `i32.ge_s` descending, inverted + `br_if`); the increment is `v += K` before the back-branch, and `break`/`continue` ride the existing loop-frame stacks. The six scratch/locals WALKERS (`collectLocals`, `blockHasMemberPopKind`/`PushKind`/`StrOp`/`MapOp`, `collectRefPushSlots`/`MemberPopRefSlots`) recurse into `frBody` (+ the bound exprs), so pushes/string-ops/maps inside `for` bodies reserve their frames. TOP-LEVEL `for`s work too: the START FUNCTION now has DECLARED LOCALS (`collectStartLocals` walks `startStmts`; `fbBeginFunc`/`fbEmitLocalsVec` take the count), and `declaredIndexOf` resolves module-scope (`fnIx -1`) names through the scope stack (index == slot — no params; a global-section constexpr init still sees an empty stack, so nothing changes there). `ForIn` stays unsupported (fails loudly). Goldens 14/14 byte-identical; runtime oracle 24 → **28** (`loops/for-sum`, `for-step`, `for-range-bound-named-step`, `arrays/basics`); 12 more programs emit (70 emit gaps remain), 8 of them currently failing at run (moved buckets — never passing before; to investigate). (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`, `tests/selfhost_wasm_emit_test.ts`)
- **H-emit PRINT + top-level statements + the RUNTIME corpus oracle (tier 2) — 24 `@run` files run byte-correct** — the `docs/selfhost-print-emit-plan.md` implementation. `wasmEmit.vl` gains (1) an IMPORT SECTION (id 2): when any `print` call exists (an arena pre-pass sets `gImports = 4`), the host's `"imports"`-module print family (`__print_i32__`/`__print_bool__`/`__print_char__` `(i32)->()`, `__print_str_flush__` `()->()`) imports at wasm function indices 0..3 — every LOCAL function's index shifts by `gImports` at the WRITE points (the one `fbCall` site, the export entries, `startFnIdx`), `fnIndices` stays the arena position; two functypes append after the user/start functypes; a print-FREE program emits no import section, so the goldens stay byte-identical BY CONSTRUCTION (14/14 held, and the gated FULL FIXPOINT 2/2 — stage2==stage3 holds with the print machinery in). (2) `print(i32)` lowers to `call 0`; the void import means a bare print statement skips the result `drop`. (3) TOP-LEVEL STATEMENTS: `collectFns` now collects non-decl statements into `startStmts` instead of failing, and the synthetic START function runs them in source order after the non-const global inits (the host's `__program__` model) — a pure-statement program (most `@run` files) emits with 0 user functions. `print(string)`/`print(bool-as-true/false)` and scratch-needing top-level statements are the next slices. NEW `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts` (tier 2): each whitelisted `@run` file is lexed/parsed/CHECKED/EMITTED by the VL pipeline, the module instantiates with the host import family, top-level statements run via the wasm start function, and the captured logs must EQUAL the file's ordered `@log` directives — **24/304 pass** (arith, calls/forward-refs/mutual recursion, while-loops, soft keywords, discarded-call). Buckets of the rest: 84 emit gaps, 77 checker false-rejects, 76 parse gaps, 28 scratch-needing statements, 11 emitter traps (bugs to pin), 4 bool-print log diffs. (`compiler/wasmEmit.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_run_test.ts`)
- **H-check Phase 2.12: LAMBDAS + function values + `.map`/`.filter` — ledger 251 → 255** — first-class functions land in the self-host front end (checker-side; emit lowering is a later slice). `parsePrimary` gains a `FUNCTION` case: a function EXPRESSION (`function(n: i32) n * 2`) parses via `parseFuncExpr` — like `parseFunc` but the name is OPTIONAL — into an anonymous `FuncDecl` (name `""`), with the same generics/bare-body shape. `checkFuncDeclNode` now RETURNS the function's own `TyFunc` (instead of void), so a `FuncDecl` in expression position evaluates to its type — `const f = inc` binds a function value (the Ident already resolved to the hoisted TyFunc), lambdas type as arguments, and anonymous functions skip the name-binding bookkeeping. Array `.map(fn)`/`.filter(pred)` type-check: the callback is a 1-arg function over the element type; `map` yields an array of the callback's RETURN type (`xs.map(function(n: i32) n > 2)` is `bool[]`), `filter` yields the receiver array type and requires a bool predicate. Re-sweep: +4 accepts (`arrays/map-filter`, `functions/function-equality`-class), rejects flat. Un-annotated function-typed PARAMS (`function apply(fn, a, b) fn(a, b)`) need param inference — deferred. (`compiler/parser.vl`, `compiler/typecheck.vl`, `tests/selfhost_corpus_test.ts`)
- **H-native CI + corpus tiers** — `ci-native`, a parallel CI job gating the native path end-to-end (vl-host build → seed → native fixpoint → native suites) (#280); tier-3 native corpus alignment — the corpus driven through the native `vl`, RUN/REJECT whitelists asserting native == host (#279).
- **H-CLI growth** — `vl build -O` via wasm-opt + `vl run <file.wasm>` passthrough (#282); `vl check` is parse+typecheck only — faster, and an emit-coverage gap is not a check failure (#283).
- **H-emit fixes** — postfix `x++`/`x--` yields the OLD value (#284); wasm `name` custom section for legible trap backtraces, gated OFF by default (#285); token source positions through the bridge (spans rung 1) (#286); struct-array `for-in` (#287); inline union params interned like named aliases (#288); `string[]` local kind inferred from a Call's return type (#302).
- **H-numerics: i64 / f64 / f32 end-to-end** — measured design (#289); i64 scalars + print (#290) and full i64 arithmetic/comparison/bitwise/shift (#291); f64 fixed end-to-end — literal/local/param/global/print + arith (#293); `f64[]` typed arrays on a distinct `(array (mut f64))` backing (#296); f32 scalars + the full widening-conversion matrix (#297). **Language rule (both compilers):** implicit numeric widening is LOSSLESS-only — `i32→i64`, `i32→f64`, `f32→f64`; the lossy `i32→f32` / `i64→f64` are rejected (host-parity test pins native == host == spec over the 4×4 matrix) (#298).
- **H-soundness floor — false-accepts closed in `typecheck.vl`** — empty/multi-codepoint char literals rejected via lexer-diag propagation (#299); un-inferable empty collection bindings rejected (#300); definite assignment — use-before-assign rejected via fork/join flow (#301); unsound `is`/union/equality programs rejected (#303); same-scope redeclaration + literal `for` step 0 rejected (#305).
- **H-lambdas: function values in the self-host emitter** — measured design: closure ABI = `{ code: (ref $fnsig), env: structref }` invoked by `call_ref` over a declarative elem segment, no table; emit-simple / optimize-with-`-O` (#304; param-skip ergonomics design #295). Slices 1–2: non-capturing lambdas + capturing (non-escaping) closures — env-leading params gated module-wide on `fnValUsed`, goldens byte-identical (#306). Slice 5: contextual callback-parameter inference — `xs.map(function(n) n*2)` pins `n: i32` from the receiver; uninferable text aligned to the host (#307).
- **H-lambdas: escaping closures + function-valued struct fields** — a closure stored in a struct field / returned from its scope works end-to-end (`escaping.vl`): kind-14 anon-struct fields, closure-cell globals, scope-aware nested-function resolution (fixing a #306 double-mapping bug), export dedup for same-named nested functions; struct/function-value `==` now fails LOUDLY instead of emitting invalid wasm (struct-equality lowering is follow-up; funcref identity needs a closure-struct token under the `call_ref` ABI) (#310).
- **F-iteration speed** — CI cargo cache keyed on the rustc version (fixes permanently-cold 2m31s wasmtime rebuilds) (#308); `scripts/refresh-compiler.sh` — ~3s seed refresh by self-compiling current `compiler/*.vl` with the existing seed, TS fallback for a stale seed, atomic replace (#309).
- **H-emit: `.map`/`.filter` HOF lowering** — the generic closure-calling loop over the #306 `call_ref` ABI (per #304: emit simple, `-O` devirtualizes); i32/f64/bool-result maps, chained `.map(…).filter(…)`, named-function callbacks (#314).
- **H-diagnostics: source positions (spans rung 2)** — native `vl check`/`build` print `path:line:col: message`; a `nodeToks` AST side-table + token-resolved `tErr`, byte-compatible diag stream so corpus `@error`/host-parity matching is untouched (#312).
- **H-maps: native rep aligned to the host (7-field) + tombstone delete** — native string-keyed maps now match `builtins/maps.ts` field-for-field — `{keys, vals, live, index, count, size, hashes}` — across two slices: 5→6-field host order + the `UPDATE_GOLDENS` re-pin infra (#315), then the 7th `hashes` cache + `m.delete(key)` (tombstone-aware probe, `size--`, amortized compaction; the BUG-1/BUG-2 churn regressions) (#316). The goldens are a native self-snapshot, validated by the FULL fixpoint + behavioral parity. (The earlier immediate-compaction approach was dropped — a goldens-dodge; the host's tombstone design is better and now matched.)
- **H-maps: `Set()` + `s.add()` + `m.get(k) ?? d`** — `Set()` emits as a boolean-valued map (same 7-field struct), `s.add` stores constant `true` via a `valConst1`-parameterized `emitMapSet` (existing paths byte-identical, no golden moved), and the `??` handler accepts a `.get()` method LHS (#317).
- **H3-tail rounds 5–7: native corpus parity DONE (sans parked xfails) — sweep 292 → 312/316** — #339 emit long-tail (null inference, index traps, list equality/concat, operator-overload returns, f64 fields, not-is narrowing); #340 function-identity equality (an `id: i32` third field on the closure fat-pointer; `==`/`\!=` over function values and function-valued fields lower as id+env identity, host-matching); #341 H3 native modules (host fetch loop behind a line-leading `import {` gate, compiler-owned graph resolution with the host's exact diagnostics, per-module `name$mN` rename, whole-program merge — single-source consumers byte-identical, fixpoint assemblies range-blank their own imports); #342 field-rep long tail (union-field ELSE-arm complement narrowing + `memberUnionReadKind` classification + multi-level member-path keys; recursive inline-shape interning — an annotation whose field is itself an inline shape now interns instead of silently colliding by field-name set; `i32[] | null` as vt kind 15 / field code 18 with `ref.as_non_null` at the narrowed-read sites that feed non-null scratch slots; map-typed struct fields as code 19 with the find-only mv-slot lookup, the recursive map-value back-edge closing inside the single shared rec group). Native = host on the tracked metric; the residue is the parked soundness xfails + the untested loud-failure long tail.
- **F-tiers (partial): corpus-primary test infra** — the native sweep parallelized over every core (`JOBS=$(nproc)`; 316 files in ~0.5s wall, buckets/summary byte-identical to the serial loop); the deno-side corpus RUN half gated off by default (`SELFHOST_DENO_RUN=1` re-enables it as a V8-vs-wasmtime bisect tool; check→emit verdicts + the functype-portability invariant stay on); `ci-native` seeds from a rolling `actions/cache` keyed on `compiler/*.vl` + the driver and self-refreshes via `refresh-compiler.sh` (~3s), demoting the ~80s TS stage-0 bootstrap to cold-cache/stale-seed fallback — the fixpoint step re-proves the refreshed seed every run. Full parallel battery: 12s (#343).
- **F-tiers (round 2): ci-job trim — `deno task test` warm 11.9s → ~2.7s, CI cold ~268s → ~100s** — profiling showed the `ci` job's cost is three ~90–107s SINGLE-UNIT compiles of assemblies containing `wasmEmit.vl` (superlinear: the same modules as a 2-module graph compile in ~5s). The two whose proofs stand elsewhere are gated to the `SELFHOST_DENO_RUN=1` bisect tier: the corpus-run remainder (check→emit verdicts + the functype invariant — verdicts live in `selfhost_corpus_test` + the native REJECT tier; the invariant is enforced de facto by every native wasmtime instantiation) and the V8-side golden fixpoint (`selfhost_emit_fixpoint_test` — the native `stage3 == stage4` proves the fixpoint over a strictly larger input every ci-native run; byte-parity-with-host was the bootstrap trust mechanism, retired with parity). The never-cached `compileProgram` graph suites (lexer/parser/typecheck/pipeline/wasm_emit, ~10–13s EVERY run) now ride a whole-compile cache (`compileProgramCached`) — 28s cold, ~0.3s warm. The goldens byte-tripwire (`selfhost_emit_program_test`) stays always-on.
- **F-tiers (round 3): the golden byte-tripwire goes NATIVE — no big assembly remains always-on** — `scripts/native-golden-check.ts` builds each golden source with the seed (`vl build` + byte-cmp against `tests/golden/*.wasm`, ~1s; `UPDATE_GOLDENS=1` re-pins) and runs in ci-native after the fixpoint; `selfhost_emit_program_test` (the last ~90–160s cold assembly in the `ci` job) joins the `SELFHOST_DENO_RUN=1` bisect tier. Two golden sources predated the checker gate and were rewritten checker-clean (g_arith summed raw booleans — the A15 coercion gap; g_maparray indexed a nullable `pop()` unguarded) and re-pinned as a reviewed act — three-way byte agreement verified (`vl build` == TS-hosted emitter == self-compiled emitter, 14/14). Default suite: 1534 passed / 1022 ignored in ~2s warm.
- **Kill-TS step 0: the corpus documents each compiler's CANONICAL message** — the wasm corpus oracle (`cases_wasm_test.ts`) skipped each case whose `@error` directive pinned the TS compiler's exact wording. Resolved by making the corpus state the compiler's REAL (clearer) diagnostic and having whichever compiler diverges opt out — never by shrinking the directive to a substring (which buried the better message). Two fronts, 37 cases off `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` (95 → 58): (1) **34 where VL's message is clearer** — the directive now carries VL's FULL message (`member access '.x' on non-object {x: i32}?`; `return type mismatch: expected i32, got string`; `` `is` check type 'boolean' is not a variant of string | i32 ``; positional `@error-at`s use VL's line:col); the wasm oracle adjudicates, and the frozen TS compiler skips them via a new `WASM_CANONICAL_WORDING` list in `cases_test.ts` — the directive-quality inverse of `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES`. (2) **3 where VL was genuinely WORSE** — VL adopts the actionable TS wording so both compilers match the rich directive: an i32-keyed `Map()`/`Set()` annotation now says `An i32-keyed Map isn't supported yet — i32 keys use a list/array (\`T[]\`); \`Map\`/\`Set\` keys must be \`string\` for now` (naming Map vs Set from the initializer) instead of `unknown type '{[i32]:i32}'`; an object literal assigned to a map now says `An object literal isn't a map value — construct a map with \`Map()\` …` instead of `cannot assign {} to 'o' of type {[string]: i32}`. Both per-side divergence lists empty out as the TS compiler retires. Verified end-to-end: wasm oracle 410/0, TS oracle 438/0 (36 skipped), native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): 29 more wording divergences carry VL's message — `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` 51 → 22, `WASM_CANONICAL_WORDING` 34 → 63** — the same treatment for the next batch where VL's diagnostic is as clear or clearer than the frozen TS compiler's: `if-condition must be bool, got i32` (vs TS `expected boolean`); `cannot compare i32 with string` (vs `expected i32, got "x"`); `wrong number of arguments: expected 1, got 2` (vs `unmatched parameter`); `no field 'plain' on {x: i32}` (vs `Unknown property \`plain\``); `for-in expects an array, got i32`; the parser comma-recovery messages; and two cases where VL helpfully emits FEWER diagnostics than TS (no cascade — `functions/trailing-comma-illegal`, `soundness/not-is-guard-…`), whose directive now matches VL's single error. The wasm oracle adjudicates all 29; the TS oracle skips them. Two VL messages were sharpened on review (maint. feedback on #377): `operator '+' expects numeric operands` → `operator '+' is not defined for i32 and string` (`+` has string/array forms, so the "numeric-only" claim was wrong), and the union member-access error `member access '.x' on non-object {A} | {B}` → `field 'x' is not on every member of {A} | {B} — narrow with \`is\` first` (the members ARE objects; the field just isn't shared). Verified: wasm oracle 439/0, TS oracle 409/0 (65 skipped), native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14. The 22 left are the genuine VL-is-worse cases (numeric lossy-conversion messages, lexer literal validation, bodyless-`type` parser recovery), real cascade bugs, and the parked soundness xfails — each a compiler fix, not a directive edit.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): VL explains lossy numeric conversions — `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` 22 → 19, both compilers now adjudicate** — a numeric assignment the lossless-only implicit-widening rule (#298) rejects (`let x: f32 = anI32`, `i64 → f64`, `f64 → i32`) reported the opaque `cannot assign i32 to 'x' of type f32`. VL now emits the same explanation the TS spec does — `i32 doesn't fit in f32 — the conversion is lossy and must be made explicit (narrowing truncates the value)` — gated on both sides being numeric at the let-decl assignability failure. Because the two compilers now word it identically, the three cases leave `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` with NO `WASM_CANONICAL_WORDING` skip — both oracles adjudicate them. Verified: wasm oracle 442/0, TS oracle 409/0, native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): the lexer validates malformed numeric literals — `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` 19 → 14** — `0xG`/`0x`/`0x_FF`/`1__0`/`1_` used to split into `0` + an identifier, surfacing the baffling `undeclared identifier 'xG'`. `lexer.vl` now recognizes a `0x`/`0b`/`0o` prefix REGARDLESS of what follows (new `scanRadix` + `isRadixDigit`) and validates the digit run and `_` separators, emitting ONE clear lex error and consuming the malformed run so nothing cascades: `hex literal has no digits after \`0x\`` (`0x`), `invalid hex digit 'G'` (`0xG`), `` `_` may only separate digits in a numeric literal `` (misplaced separators, incl. plain-decimal `1__0`/`1_`). Four match the TS spec verbatim → both oracles adjudicate; `0xG` improves on the TS host's two-error cascade to one clean diagnostic → `cases_test` skips it (`WASM_CANONICAL_WORDING`). (Gotcha pinned in a code comment: the frozen TS host emitter DROPS a bare boolean call used directly as an `if`/`while` condition — store it in a bool local / lead with a comparison, as the rest of the lexer does.) Verified: full `deno task test` 2007/0, native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): a bodyless `type` alias reports cleanly — `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` 14 → 12** — `type Bar` with no `= body` (then `let y: Bar = 0`) cascaded three parse errors (`expected EQUAL but found NEWLINE`, …) because the parser swallowed the next line as the body. `parser.vl` now detects the missing `=` and emits ONE actionable error AT the declaration — `A type alias requires a body; write \`type Bar = …\` to define \`Bar\`` (the TS spec's wording, verbatim) — recovering as a sentinel `TypeDecl` (`tdFields == [-1]`) that `typecheck.vl` pass 0a registers as the error type, so the use site doesn't cascade either (the error type suppresses the assignment check, as an undeclared-type let already does). Both compilers now adjudicate the two cases (`types/bodyless-alias`, `types/recursive-type`). Verified: full `deno task test` 2009/0, `deno lint`/`deno check` clean, native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): five spurious-cascade diagnostics cleaned up — `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` 12 → 7** — each emitted a correct first error followed by a redundant SECOND one; the cascade is now suppressed so the corpus sees one clean diagnostic. (1) **never-typed bindings** — `isNeverTy` now recognizes an empty union (a degenerate `type D = D` or empty intersection `A & B`) as `never`, and the let-decl assignment check skips a `never` annotation, so `let d: D = …` no longer adds `cannot assign … of type ` (empty) after the declaration already reported the never type (`types/never-value-intersection`, `never-value-self-intersection`, `self-alias-still-clean`). (2) **uninferable lambda param** — an un-annotated lambda param with no contextual type reports once (`cannot infer a type for parameter \`n\``); the follow-on `cannot infer a return type` is suppressed since the param failure is the root cause (`functions/lambda-uninferable-param`). (3) **malformed object-literal key** — a doubled comma `{ x: 1,, y }` reported `expected IDENT but found COMMA` then cascaded three more from leftover tokens; the parser now recovers by skipping to the next `,`/`}` and names what it wanted (`expected a field name but found \`,\``, not the internal token kind) (`objects/trailing-comma-illegal`). On review (maint. #380), the never-value messages also regained the TS host's source-level DETAIL — a new `operandKind`/`splitTopAmp` describes the two operands (`Type \`C\` is \`never\` (an empty type — \`A & B\`, an object and a string literal, have no common values)…`), and a self-reference is called out specifically (`Type \`E\` refers to itself inside an intersection (\`A & E\`), so it reduces to \`never\``); the empty-intersection lint/never cases pick up the same detail. With VL now matching the TS wording verbatim, `never-value-intersection`/`-self-intersection`/`self-alias`/`lambda` + the two empty-intersection cases all adjudicate on BOTH oracles; only `objects/trailing-comma` keeps a `WASM_CANONICAL_WORDING` skip. Verified: full `deno task test` 2015/0, `deno lint`/`deno check` clean, native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): two i64-width emitter gaps closed — `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` 7 → 5 (and the wasm oracle now dual-adjudicates the whole corpus bar 5 cases)** — the native emitter produced INVALID wasm on two value-preserving i32→i64 widenings the checker permits; both now lower correctly. (1) An un-annotated `const m = n` from an i64 initializer materialized an i32 local — `letIsI64` now infers i64 from the initializer (`exprIsI64`) inside a function, like `letIsF64` does for floats, threaded through all five call sites (globals stay annotation-only to avoid cross-global recursion) (`numerics/i64-infer-let`). (2) An i32 VARIABLE argument into an i64 parameter passed unextended — the call site now widens it via the existing `emitExprAsI64` (`i64.extend_i32_s`), mirroring the f64/f32 arg paths (`numerics/widen-call-arg`). Both are `@run` cases, so both oracles now execute them and match `@log` output. Remaining `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` (5): the ref-element `__array_new__` long tail (fails loudly, never wrong bytes), the `soundness/README` prose-as-`@run` quirk, and 3 parked soundness xfails. Verified: full `deno task test` 2017/0, `deno lint`/`deno check` clean, native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): ref-element `__array_new__` lands — `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` 5 → 4 (the wasm oracle now adjudicates the whole corpus bar a prose quirk + 3 parked xfails)** — `__array_new__(n, "s")` and `__array_new__(n, {…})` (string- and struct-element fills) used to fail loudly (i32/boolean/f64 only). The native emitter now lowers them: `arrNewIntrKind` gained kind 2 (string) / kind 3 (struct-ref), threaded through the classifiers (`exprStringArray`/`exprRefArray` claim the call; `exprArray` no longer does), the collection (`refListElemNameOfExpr` names the fill's struct so `rlInternName` interns its ref-list slot), and the emit (`array.new` of the string backing `mkArrIdx`/`mkListIdx` or the interned ref backing `rlBackIdx`/`rlWrapIdx`, with the `{…}` fill seeded by `pendingStructIdx`; a non-null element ref stores into the nullable backing slot directly). `@run` case, so both oracles execute it (`hi`/`hi`/`5`). Remaining `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES` (4): `soundness/README` (a prose line parses as `@run`) + 3 parked soundness xfails. Verified: full `deno task test` 2018/0, `deno lint`/`deno check` clean, native fixpoint holds, goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 0 (cont.): the .vl LINT PASS joins the wasm corpus oracle — the last structural reason the TS oracle existed is gone** — the wasm oracle used to SKIP every `@warning`/`@info`/`@hint`-only case (24 of them) because the self-hosted compiler exposed no lint tier. Now `compiler/lint.vl` is part of the compiler assembly (refresh/native-fixpoint/build-compiler-wasm) and the driver exposes `lintSrc()` + per-code-point `lintSev*`/`lintMsg*` accessors over lint.vl's own `lintCount`/`lintLineAt`/`lintColAt`; `cases_wasm_test` runs the lint pass on every clean single-file case and adjudicates the lint directives strict-by-default (matching `cases_test`). The bulk of the lint corpus (unused-binding/-function, prefer-const, dead/constant-branch, step-0) is now dual-adjudicated. Integration snag fixed: lint.vl's `diverges` collided with a LOCAL `let diverges` in typecheck.vl, tripping the seed's capture analysis (top-level function names are excluded from a lambda's captures, so the shadowed local mis-resolved → "captured variable not found"); renamed to `stmtDiverges` (the underlying capScan-shadowing bug is tracked separately). Five cases stay `EXPECTED_DIVERGENCES`: three type-aware `unreachable: exhaustive is-chain` @infos and an empty-range @warning (the native lint is parse-only and the driver's diag stream is error-only, so checker-derived warnings/infos aren't visible yet — a later slice), plus a checker gap (`generic-intersection-no-warn` reports `unknown type 'T&S'`). Verified: full `deno task test` 2027/0, wasm oracle 466/0, `deno lint`/`deno check` clean, native fixpoint holds (seed now contains lint.vl, 575145 bytes), goldens 14/14.
- **Kill-TS step 1: the .vl FORMATTER joins the compiler — `compiler/format.vl` ports the host `compiler/format.ts`** — an AST-driven formatter walks the parser arena (`P.nodes`) and regenerates canonical VL source: expressions are printed structurally (operator spacing, precedence-minimal parens, list reflow at width-80, multi-line trailing-comma policy), while TYPE syntax (variable / parameter / return annotations, `is` check-types, `type`/union decls — stored as synthetic NAME strings in the arena) is sliced VERBATIM from source via the per-node `[start, stop)` spans (`pos` .. `nodeEndOf`); control flow, blocks, and statement sequencing are pretty-printed with own-line vs trailing comment placement (the lexer's retained `//`/`///` trivia), blank-line preservation, and one-line `if` collapse. `format.vl` is part of the compiler assembly (refresh/native-fixpoint/build-compiler-wasm) and the driver exposes `formatSrc()` + `fmtLen`/`fmtByteAt` per-code-point accessors. A new `tests/selfhost_format_test.ts` drives the module graph over 15 representative snippets and asserts byte-for-byte equality with the host `format()`. Self-host emitter gaps surfaced and worked around in the port: a bare member-call (`.slice`) as a function's TAIL/statement expression is rejected (`unsupported member-call statement`) — bound to a `let` first; an `if x == 0 { … }` whose body REASSIGNS (no `else`) trips a frozen-host type quirk — led with a different comparison; and a TRANSITIVE forward reference does not resolve — the binary-chain cluster is ordered callee-before-caller. Divergences from the host (intrinsic to the self-host AST, documented in the file header): explicit `Paren` nodes are kept (the host drops them); compound assignment is reprinted in its desugared `a = a + b` form. Verified: `deno task test` 2044/0, native fixpoint holds (seed now contains format.vl, 607985 bytes), goldens 14/14, `deno lint`/`deno check` clean.

- **fix: a UNION OF LITERAL UNIONS is a literal union — the flatten lands in the CHECKER, because the filing's "only the render has to move" is 12 cells DOWN** — `K | K2` repped as a bare string with `is K` const-folded to `i32.const 0`, a guard that can never fire: 13 of its 20 grid cells broken, 11 SILENTLY. `docs/internals/litunion-compact-rep-design.md` §8.3 handed the slice off as render-only, and that half is REFUTED by measurement — `tsToTyReal`'s annotation-union arm (and its `nameToTyReal` twin) never flattened a union MEMBER, so `K | K2` interned as a `TyUnion` OF UNIONS; `tyIsLitUnion` is structural over `TyLit` members and answered NO for the whole annotation, which made both renderers soften it AND left `anyLitUnionUsed` at 0, switching off every atom classifier in the MODULE (the tell: adding one unrelated `const forceflag: K` fixed a field read three functions away). (ii) Applying that UNCONDITIONALLY over-reaches: `isVariantSpelling` answers about the tested TYPE, not the receiver, so `v is {meow: i32}` in a `g<T>` instance where `T` is `i32` also stopped folding — **12 of 60 monomorphized-guard cells DOWN**, found only because the second grid varies the axis the rule does not mention. The gate is scoped by `nodeTyIsUnion(receiver)`, the CHECKER's view rather than the emitter's `exprUnion` (the REP classification, which is exactly what has the gap). Grids: `is`-spelling × registration-source × read-position = 96 cells, **2 UP** (silent INVALID WASM → clean emit reject), 0 down; the delete-the-bystander control (inferred return removed) is **0 of 96**; the monomorphized grid is **0 of 60**. **THE RESIDUE IS RE-ATTRIBUTED AND RULED**: 6 SOURCE-spells-it-twice (the file writes the inline shape AND the named chain, deliberately) + 3 UCOLL where the second row IS the union's second member and carries the tag `x is Dog` needs + 4 UCOLL-in-kind where `unVarStart`/`unVarCount` slice `uVariants` per union + 2 UEXP that are **canon's, not the walk's** — ZZLTN reads `src={t:i32,a:i32}|{t:i32,b:i32}|null nom=AB|null reg=…structural`, i.e. the ARENA renderer gets `AB|null` right and `canonEmitName` expands it structurally because `unionAliasMembers` renders through `tyToEmitName`, which makes `types/nullable-union-alias.vl`'s own header stale; filed, not taken, as it is a canon union-arm change in the twice-ruled region. **ALSO FILED WITH ITS ROOT CAUSE**: an UN-ANNOTATED array literal of union elements is silent INVALID WASM on master in every shape measured (`[pick(c)]`, `[w]` for any union binding, even a plainly DECLARED `type U = A | B` value) because `arrLitIsRef` classifies by the SYNTAX of the first element and the checker's `nodeArrayElemName` has no `TyUnion` arm; `unions/error-inline-shape-is-over-inferred-list-element.vl` pins the diagnostic half and is the corpus's one witness that **BUILDMSG is not an optional channel** — both compilers exit build rc 1 and run nonzero, and the improvement shows on exactly one of six. Corpus A/B 1,712 files × 6 channels: **2 rows, both the branch's own fixtures, no cell down** (against master's own 1,710 it is 0 diffs everywhere). Fuzz A/B 6,000 cases/side byte-identical. Three sabotages: THIRDOFF and UNSCOPED are the two halves of one rule, each reddening the file the other leaves green (2 failures apiece), and GATEBACK is green **by design** on a suite that cannot read a row count — verified live on the byte channel. **+51 bytes.** (`docs/internals/destringify-types-program.md`, `tests/cases/functions/inferred-union-one-row.vl`, `tests/cases/unions/error-inline-shape-is-over-inferred-list-element.vl`, `tests/cases/generics/mono-static-is-inline-shape-beside-union.vl`)
